    movel(p[-0.7228, -0.5890, 0.1700, 0.0000, 3.1416, 0.0000], a=0.2, v=0.04)
    
        local q_now = get_actual_joint_positions()
        local target_w3 = q_now[5] + (-0.1185)
        if target_w3 > -6.161012259539984 and target_w3 < 6.161012259539984 then
            q_now[5] = target_w3
